The CTest probe `test_validate_posix_build_type` uses a cached Debug fixture and fake CMake/CTest commands to verify the Release reconfiguration path; keep hosted POSIX release evidence required.

- Windows package-path binding slice #4301 under #3873: `apps/copperfin_runtime_host/main.cpp::package_path_component_equal()` must use `CompareStringOrdinal(..., TRUE)` over native `std::wstring` components on Windows, not locale-dependent `std::towlower`; retain exact `left == right` on POSIX. This governs package-root rebinding callers while preserving traversal, physical containment, reparse rejection, UTF-8 public path strings, and machine contracts. Do not promise composed/decomposed Unicode normalization, and do not change unrelated transaction identity helpers. Keep explicit UTF-8 Unicode case-variant audit coverage under the Windows test block.
- Managed Studio-host stream slice #4288 under #2348: `CopperfinStudioHostBridge.CreateProcessStartInfo()` must set `StandardOutputEncoding` and `StandardErrorEncoding` to `Encoding.UTF8` whenever redirected output is enabled. This shared factory serves VSIX and standalone host calls; preserve JSON keys, enum values, diagnostics, process exit behavior, localization environment propagation, and non-redirected launches. Keep managed coverage with a non-ASCII path and leave native UTF-8 JSON emission unchanged.
- Protocol-1 debug value framing slice #4300 under #110: native `copperfin_runtime_host` must encode only the line-oriented VFP value fields it owns (`debug.global.*`, `debug.frame[*].local.*`, `debug.watch.expression`, `debug.watch.value`, and `debug.watch.error`) with backslash-v1 escapes for backslash, CR, LF, and tab. The shared `CopperfinRuntimeDebugClient` used by VSIX and standalone Studio must decode those fields, while leaving path, command, identity, and other unencoded debug fields unchanged so legacy single-line values are not reinterpreted. Preserve response begin/end framing, invariant keys, watch/local/global underlying values, batch and persistent debug behavior, and localized human diagnostics; keep native multiline and managed parser regressions aligned.
- Windows generated-launcher trust slice #4299 under #4041: the configured public launcher OUTFILE is a native `copperfin_launcher_guard.exe` copy, while the published managed apphost must be renamed to the stable package-relative `Copperfin.GeneratedLauncher.apphost.exe`. The guard must select `app.cfdebug` when present, otherwise `app.cfmanifest`; parse the existing `launcher_artifact=` inventory; verify direct, regular, physically contained files and SHA-256 bytes for the public guard, internal apphost, DLL, deps, and runtimeconfig before `CreateProcessW`; reject missing, replaced, redirected/reparse-backed, duplicate, or wrongly classified records before managed code starts. Preserve the configured public filename, package-relative inventory/provenance, versioned manifest/debug fields, shell-free forwarding, invariant exit codes/status fields, and explicit `--locale`/`/locale` localization through the shared catalogs. Unsupported non-Windows launcher paths remain unchanged, and hosted Windows process/tamper evidence is required before closing #4299.
- Managed FRX/LBX geometry slice #4297 under #24/#2348: `CopperfinDesignSurfaceControl` must parse VFP `HPOS`, `VPOS`, `WIDTH`, and `HEIGHT` text with `NumberStyles.Float` and `CultureInfo.InvariantCulture`; native asset values use invariant decimal points even when the UI culture is pt-BR or another comma-decimal locale. Preserve rounded layout behavior, shared report/label model fields, machine contracts, and the pt-BR fractional-geometry smoke regression.
- Windows UTF-8 package/query follow-up #4295 under #3873/#3866: packaged `.qpr` files are immutable query/file-byte assets, not source-text assets. The runtime host must admit their bytes through `verified_file_bytes` so strict RowSourceType=4 loading and Requery remain stable after physical replacement. Any UTF-8 package transaction temporary path must be rebuilt with `path_from_utf8_string(...)` after string concatenation; implicit `std::filesystem::path(std::string)` construction is lossy on Windows. Preserve manifest asset identities, strict fail-closed behavior, non-strict pathname reads, and the existing machine contracts. Focused host audit and verified-DBF tests pass locally; the aggregate audit harness has an unrelated long-running debug-document subprocess, and the new exact-head Windows gate remains required.
- Strict local VCX security slice #4298 under #3866/#4239: `NEWOBJECT("ClassName", "library.vcx", ...)` must consume admitted immutable VCX/VCT bytes through the shared verified xAsset snapshot before `studio::open_document()` parses the class library. Preserve the logical resolved `ClassLibrary` path, constructor/property/method behavior, non-strict pathname loading, localized `NewObjectVcxOpenFailed` failures, `prg.object.newobject` telemetry, and the heap-backed iterative frame machine. The shared snapshot helper may use the logical memo-sidecar candidate when its admitted bytes exist even if the physical sidecar was replaced or removed; never reopen the accepted VCX/VCT path in strict mode. Focused procedure-class security coverage proves tampered physical bytes are ignored and missing admission fails closed; hosted cross-platform validation is still required.
- Persistent PRG debugger slice #4296 under #110: the runtime host's `--debug-server` is protocol version 1, emits `debug.server.protocol: 1` followed by `debug.server.ready: true`, reads one invariant command per stdin line, and frames each response with `debug.response.begin`/`debug.response.end`. The shared managed VSIX/standalone client must retain one live process for Start/Continue/Step/Next/Out, send `exit` during deterministic shutdown, dispose/rematerialize transient debug manifests, and preserve the existing batch `--debug-command` CLI as a compatibility path. Human error lines remain catalog-localized; readiness, response markers, command identities, pause reasons, and machine fields remain invariant. Keep startup side effects exactly-once under managed sequential commands, preserve #4262 entry-stop semantics, and keep the heap-backed iterative runtime frame machine stack-frugal. Hosted Windows VSIX/debug validation is still required before closing #4296.
- Cross-platform UTF-8 boundary follow-up under #3873/#4245/#4249: any PRG/project/asset path stored as UTF-8 text must pass through `path_from_utf8_string(...)` before native filesystem inspection or construction, including `is_regular_file`, output/home-directory classification, companion staging, and visual-asset undo/extension helpers. Convert native paths back with `path_to_utf8_string(...)` for identity, diagnostics, and machine contracts. POSIX-rooted `FULLPATH()` inputs are portable VFP syntax and must be lexically normalized as strings rather than parsed as host Windows root-relative paths. Preserve package, debug, asset, localization, and JSON contracts; hosted Windows evidence remains required for #4244/#4245/#4249.
- Asset-inspection follow-up under #3873/#4269: index probes must reuse the native path for both stream opening and file sizing; never call `std::filesystem::file_size(...)` on the UTF-8 text path. Convert filesystem failures into the existing localized open diagnostic and preserve companion-index behavior and asset/JSON contracts across hosts.
- Asset-inspection follow-up under #3873/#4269: DBC export must resolve the `.dct` sidecar from the converted native DBC path, not by implicitly constructing `std::filesystem::path` from the UTF-8 string. Keep non-ASCII basenames in inspection/export fixtures so Windows companion resolution is actually covered; preserve returned UTF-8 identities and JSON contracts.
- Asset-inspection follow-up under #3873/#4269: CDX header parsing must reuse the native path for both stream opening and file sizing; never call `std::filesystem::file_size(...)` on the UTF-8 text path. Preserve localized probe diagnostics, index metadata, and companion inspection behavior across hosts.
- DBF sidecar follow-up under #3873/#4269: all `dbf_table.cpp` memo-sidecar resolution and rollback filesystem calls must pass through native paths derived by `path_from_utf8_string(...)`; do not let a UTF-8 sidecar name reach an implicit narrow `std::filesystem::path` conversion. Preserve sidecar ambiguity diagnostics, memo bytes, rollback behavior, and table contracts.
- Generated DLL/FLL bridge lifecycle slice #4289: generated wrappers must derive per-invocation request, response, stdout, and stderr artifact paths from a process-plus-sequence identity, remove stale companions before writing the request, write the request before launching `copperfin_runtime_host`, skip launch when request preparation fails, and clean all transport/log artifacts after response capture. Preserve shell-free Windows/POSIX launch behavior, bridge JSON fields, diagnostics, exit codes, and the generated-wrapper compile/build smoke coverage.
- POSIX audit-containment build slice #4290: `test_runtime_host_audit_containment` uses shared DBF fixture helpers and must keep `cf_vfp_assets` in its target link list. Preserve the focused CTest and clean `scripts/validate-posix.sh` coverage; this is a test-target dependency repair and must not alter runtime package or audit contracts.
- The FoxPro completion-set title is now catalog-backed under #2348/#4282: keep `FoxProCompletionSetContract.Identity` as the invariant `CopperfinFoxPro` machine identity, route `DisplayNameKey` through `CopperfinLocalization`, and preserve completion filtering/items across en-US, es-419, pt-BR, and qps-ploc. New Visual Studio language-service UI strings must follow this catalog/test pattern rather than adding literals.
- VSIX FoxPro IntelliSense localization now uses `CopperfinLocalization.FromVisualStudioUiCulture()`: explicit `COPPERFIN_UI_LOCALE`/`COPPERFIN_LOCALE` overrides remain authoritative, otherwise completion descriptions, quick info, signatures, project-member fallback text, and the completion-set title follow `CultureInfo.CurrentUICulture`. Keep syntax tokens, insertion text, completion kinds, signature content, and machine identifiers invariant.
- Standalone Studio startup arguments are parsed by the shared managed `CopperfinStudioStartupArguments` contract under #25/#4281: preserve positional paths and `--locale`, accept optional `--path`, `--object-name`, and `--unique-id` only for one SCX/VCX target, reject duplicate/missing/unknown/unsupported selectors with catalog-backed diagnostics, and thread selectors through `CopperfinStudioSnapshotClient`/`CopperfinStudioHostBridge.BuildArguments` without changing existing callers.
- Initial form/class startup selectors are now resolved in the shared `open_document()` model under #25/#4280: for SCX/VCX requests, resolve `UNIQUEID` before `OBJNAME`, use `NAME` only as the object-name fallback, reject ambiguous matches, and load the full table when a selector is present so targets beyond the eight-record preview remain selectable. Preserve report/label selection behavior and host JSON keys.
- Studio-host process launches are now a Windows UTF-8 boundary under #110/#3873/#4279: `execute_launch_command()` must convert UTF-8 paths/arguments to wide strings and use `CreateProcessW`; do not restore narrow `_spawnvp` for Windows. Preserve POSIX fork/exec behavior, shell-quote display strings, exit codes, argument order, and the Unicode-directory fixture.
- Generated native DLL/FLL bridge launches are now a shell-free security boundary under #110/#4278: preserve direct `CreateProcessW` on Windows and `fork`/`execve` on POSIX, with native working-directory, environment, stdout/stderr, and exit-code handling. Do not restore `std::system`, shell command construction, or platform-specific quoting as a launcher mechanism; retain the direct loaded-library metacharacter regression and keep package/debug JSON contracts unchanged.
- External-process policy slice `#4277` under `#110/#3873` routes Windows `SearchPathW` results, allowed roots, Authenticode checks, and publisher version-resource paths through `path_to_utf8_string`/`path_from_utf8_string`; generic path comparison must normalize separators only after lossless UTF-8 conversion. Preserve Windows-only authorization semantics, localized diagnostics, and the Unicode fixture that disables signature enforcement only for the copied-system-executable test.
- VSIX project-insight containment slice `#4276` under `#25/#3873` now shares `CopperfinDocumentPathIdentity.IsWithinRoot` and Windows-root detection with PJX activation. Reject rooted and traversal `RelativePath`/`Name` values before reading project files, normalize slash styles, preserve valid nested entries and insight schemas, and retain managed coverage proving outside PRGs contribute no symbols or tasks.
- Studio UTF-8 document-model slice `#4275` under `#3873` converts UTF-8 request paths to native paths before sidecar resolution and converts full primary, sidecar, memo, and diagnostic paths back through `path_to_utf8_string`. Keep extension-only parsing ASCII-safe, preserve actual casefolded entry spelling and document JSON fields, and retain the inferred/direct non-ASCII sidecar regression.
- Studio-host test portability follow-up for `#4275/#3973`: Unicode recovery fixtures must pass both input paths and expected sidecar paths through `path_to_utf8_string`, never locale-dependent `std::filesystem::path::string()`. This keeps the test exercising the native UTF-8 contract on Windows instead of corrupting its own fixture paths; ordinary ASCII fixture conversions may retain existing behavior.
- Runtime manifest slice `#4274` fixes the reader side of the shipped `backslash-v1` encoding: `quote_manifest_value()` escapes backslash and pipe, `unescape_manifest_value()` runs before field parsing, and `split_pipe()` must then consume `\\` and `\\|` as literal field content rather than treating escaped pipes as delimiters. Preserve asset/data/extension-payload field counts, machine-readable error identities, and cross-platform behavior; keep a Windows-valid metadata regression because `|` cannot be used in Windows filenames.
- Managed E3 model slice `#4273` under `#24` makes `CopperfinStudioReportLayoutObject.SectionObjectIndex` nullable. Native Studio intentionally emits `sectionObjectIndex: null` for valid unplaced FRX/LBX objects; preserve that null through deserialization and the unplaced tray, while placed-object numeric indexes and the JSON property name remain unchanged.
- E3 label-object slice `#4291` under `#24` preserves the raw FRX/LBX `PICTURE` field only for OBJTYPE=5 label objects, including field and memo provenance. Host JSON may add invariant `picture`, provenance, and `pictureAlignment` fields; map empty to `left`, `@I` to `center`, and `@J` to `right`, while retaining `other` values and never reinterpreting image/object `PICTURE` fields. The managed VSIX report-layout DTO must retain those additive fields, and its shared property grid exposes editable `PICTURE` only when the selected report object has OBJTYPE=5; the display label must be catalog-backed in en-US, es-419, pt-BR, and qps-ploc. Keep set/clear round trips, unsupported-field preservation, managed deserialization, and label/non-label selection coverage aligned with the native/host contract.
- Strict PRG source-load localization slice `#4271` under `#2348/#3866` routes missing verified main and `#INCLUDE` sources through `Runtime.Prg.Parser.Error.VerifiedSourceUnavailable` and `.VerifiedIncludeSourceUnavailable`. Preserve the path placeholder, strict fail-closed behavior, parser tokens, and runtime machine contracts; keep all four catalogs aligned and remember that initial missing-main failures may propagate as exceptions before a paused runtime state exists.
- Project-asset UTF-8 slice `#4272` under `#3873` replaces the three remaining resolved-path `.string()` conversions in `resolve_project_item_source()` with `path_to_utf8_string(...)`. Keep case-folded resolution, parent-traversal containment, staging/debug paths, and package contracts unchanged; future Windows Unicode project tests should exercise source existence and manifest paths.
- VSIX editor-pane localization slice `#4270` under `#2348` now passes `CopperfinLocalization.FromCurrentUiCulture()` into the shared editor control. Keep the actual Visual Studio pane aligned with `CultureInfo.CurrentUICulture` so localized VS instances do not show mixed-language editor chrome; standalone Studio and explicit managed controls must continue using their environment/explicit locale paths, and machine contracts plus catalog keys remain invariant.
- Standalone asset reveal is now cross-platform under #26/#4264: `CopperfinFileManager` keeps Windows Explorer `/select` behavior, uses `open --reveal` on macOS, and uses `xdg-open` on the containing directory on Linux. Keep the localized `AssetEditor.RevealInExplorerButton` wording platform-neutral and preserve quiet failure when no desktop opener exists.
- PJX workspace entries are now an MVP open/edit path under #25/#4265: the shared editor must resolve only supported child assets beneath the PJX directory, reject traversal/missing/unsupported targets, and invoke the host-owned open callback on Enter or double-click. Standalone Studio routes the callback through normalized document-tab identity; the VSIX pane delegates to Visual Studio document hosting. Keep the path allowlist and machine contracts invariant.

- Strict PRG query-file slice `#4295`: after resolving a `.qpr` path through the UTF-8 runtime working-directory boundary, strict sessions should return the admitted query bytes directly from `verified_file_byte_overrides`. This avoids a Windows narrow-path temporary snapshot dependency while preserving fail-closed behavior when bytes are not admitted, physical-file replacement resistance, and ordinary non-strict pathname reads. The Windows #4065 content-admission fixture must construct superscript device-name paths with `path_from_utf8_string(...)`, not implicit narrow `std::filesystem::path` conversion.
- Runtime package UTF-8 boundary follow-up under #3873/#4293/#4294: RuntimePackagePlan path fields are UTF-8 contract strings, but every filesystem API and transaction/manifest/archive helper must receive `path_from_utf8_string(...)` paths. Do not rely on implicit `std::filesystem::path(std::string)` construction on Windows; it uses the active narrow code page and produces mojibake for non-ASCII project/output roots. Studio workspace path helpers must use the shared path helpers for the same reason. Preserve UTF-8 package/debug JSON and manifest path fields.
- Managed read-only visual-asset slice `#4263` now preserves the native `document.readOnly` capability result: `CopperfinStudioSnapshotClient.TryLoad` must not force `--read-only` on ordinary snapshot loads. The managed report/label property-grid selections and mutation handlers must keep read-only assets non-editable, while writable-copy smoke fixtures add POSIX owner-write permission explicitly. Preserve the existing `readOnly` JSON field and explicit `--read-only` launch option; do not localize or rename machine fields.

- Durable report-layout note: FRX/LBX document titles remain filename-based display text, but the shared snapshot and host JSON now expose the header record's `NAME` field ordinal and memo block when that title slot exists. Keep null/zero provenance for synthetic or legacy assets without a header `NAME` field, and keep the managed report-layout model aligned with the additive native fields.

- Runtime-host path boundary slice `#4249` preserves UTF-8 runtime-host strings through validation and staging by converting them with `path_from_utf8_string(...)` before native filesystem operations. Keep automatic discovery, environment overrides, explicit paths, security validation, and package/debug fields unchanged; retain the Unicode package-path regression and require hosted Windows evidence before closing #3873.

- Strict PRG data reader slice `#4250` converts `RESTORE FROM` and typed `APPEND FROM` source strings through `path_from_utf8_string(...)` before extension resolution, working-directory joining, and verified-byte lookup. Preserve ordinary reads, strict fail-closed behavior, localized diagnostics, and runtime/event contracts; keep the Unicode verified-reader regression and hosted Windows evidence under #3873/#3866.

- Native lifecycle slice `#4251` dispatches Form/FormSet `Unload` in the existing iterative release order after contained objects and `Destroy`. Preserve stack-frugal frame execution, contained Form-before-FormSet ordering, `Destroy`/`Release`/`NODEFAULT` semantics, and existing telemetry; the additive `prg.object.unload` event is covered by the focused runtime test.
- Native lifecycle slice `#4284` dispatches Form/FormSet `QueryUnload` only for the supported `QUIT` path, in contained-object order before the existing iterative Destroy/Unload release sequence. A false/zero result or `NODEFAULT` vetoes QUIT and leaves the object tree live; direct form `Release` and `RELEASE` command paths must continue to skip QueryUnload. Preserve the additive `prg.object.queryunload`/`prg.object.queryunload_veto` telemetry, stack-frugal frame execution, and all existing release contracts.
- Native event-dispatch slice `#4292` under #3217 is now proven by a nested source -> native bridge method -> second native event -> object handler regression. Preserve exactly-once dispatch, stacked `AEVENTS(..., 0)` source/event/type metadata, qualified `prg.event.delegate` telemetry, and cleanup of `active_native_event_keys` after success or faults; direct BINDEVENT/RAISEEVENT and property-event paths remain unchanged, and no recursive native-stack path should be introduced.
- Environment hardening audit slice `#4287` found that the generated POSIX runtime bridge still iterates process-global `environ` directly while constructing the child environment for `execve`. Treat this as a separate concrete race defect under #110/#4010/#4278; do not assume the centralized environment/path helpers cover that generated bridge snapshot boundary.
- #4287 implementation adds a bounded mutex around each generated bridge's POSIX environment snapshot, deterministic last-wins duplicate-key normalization, a focused generated-launch test seam, and the durable contract at `docs/runtime/native-bridge-environment-contract.md`. Direct POSIX callers that mutate `environ` concurrently remain responsible for using the documented external serialization contract; no Windows path changes.
- The generated bridge test hook is intentionally emitted outside the platform-specific Windows/POSIX environment helper branches. Keep the `COPPERFIN_RUNTIME_BRIDGE_TEST_HOOKS` guard available to both Windows DLL and POSIX shared-library smoke builds; it is test-only and must not expand production export surfaces.
- Standalone Studio UTF-8 argument coverage must compare the JSON-escaped path representation on Windows and build Unicode fixture paths with `platform::path_from_utf8_string`; raw backslash-containing paths are not present verbatim inside JSON strings.
- Windows environment/path validation reproduced a test-only failure in `test_build_host_output`: the shell-safety fixture recursively copied a complete package into a longer adversarial path and failed before loading the DLL. The fixture now moves the completed package instead, preserving the shell-safety assertion while avoiding Windows copy/path-length fragility.
- Native focus-validation slice `#4256` under `#3217` dispatches the previous focusable control's `Valid` event before `LostFocus`. An explicit false/zero result must keep the current control active and suppress `LostFocus`, active-control mutation, and destination `GotFocus`; accepted validation preserves the existing order. Treat an empty event result as the default accepted path, consume any `NODEFAULT` flag, and keep nested validation calls on the iterative PRG frame machine.
- Strict XML cursor-input slice `#4261` under `#3866` routes file-backed `XMLTOCURSOR()` through the verified-byte callback. In strict sessions, admitted XML bytes are authoritative even if the logical path is absent on disk, missing admissions fail with `Runtime.Prg.RuntimeSurface.Warning.XmlToCursorVerifiedBytesUnavailable`, and newly materialized internal cursor DBF bytes must be registered before strict opening. Preserve ordinary non-strict file reads, logical XML paths, localized warning catalogs, and the stack-frugal runtime boundary.
- Strict `FILETOSTR()` slice `#4266` under `#3866` routes file reads through the verified-byte callback. In strict sessions, admitted bytes are authoritative even after the physical file changes, and missing admissions return an empty value with `Runtime.Prg.RuntimeSurface.Warning.FileToStrVerifiedBytesUnavailable`; non-strict reads remain pathname-based. Preserve the localized warning catalogs, `FERROR()` behavior, and the stack-frugal runtime boundary.
- Strict read-only `FOPEN()` slice `#4267` under `#3866` creates an immutable memory-backed handle from admitted bytes. `FREAD()`, `FGETS()`, `FSEEK()`, `FTELL()`, `FEOF()`, `FFLUSH()`, and `FCLOSE()` must stay on that buffer in strict mode; missing admission returns the existing open failure and `FERROR()` code, while write/read-write and non-strict handles retain pathname behavior. Do not add write-through semantics to this slice.
- Numeric parity slice `#4268` under `#3217` routes negative `SQRT()` inputs through `Runtime.Prg.Numeric.Error.NonNegativeArgumentRequired`; preserve `SQRT(0)` and positive results, and keep the domain diagnostic localized across all four catalogs. Do not return zero for invalid negative inputs or alter machine contracts.
- Asset-inspector UTF-8 slice `#4269` under `#3873` routes production VFP asset reads and companion-path conversions through `path_from_utf8_string()`/`path_to_utf8_string()`. Preserve case-folded companion resolution, asset/index/memo contracts, diagnostics, and JSON paths; keep Unicode asset coverage in `test_vfp_assets`.
- Debug entry-stop slice `#4262` under `#110` adds the opt-in invariant CLI flag `--debug-stop-on-entry`. The runtime host maps it to the existing `RuntimeSessionOptions.stop_on_entry` contract, and the VSIX persists the mode through every replay so Start, Continue, and step commands see the same initial entry pause. Preserve default host behavior when the flag is absent, keep pause reasons and machine fields unchanged, and retain native entry-then-step plus managed fake-host coverage.

- VSIX localization slice `#4252` routes project-workspace build-plan booleans through `AssetEditor.Summary.Boolean.True` and `.False`. Keep `DebugEnabled`, `EncryptEnabled`, `SaveCode`, and `NoLogo` as invariant managed booleans and preserve all snapshot/JSON contracts; maintain key parity across en-US, es-419, pt-BR, and qps-ploc, with pseudo-locale coverage preventing raw `True`/`False` display leakage.

- Start from live GitHub issue state plus the current repo guidance in `agents.md` and `docs/23-phase-a-dependency-breakdown.md`.
- Use the issue hierarchy: umbrella issues are planning/tracking units, parent/lane issues group work, and prompt-sized child issues are execution units.
- Shipped installer CPack verifier slice `#4235`: CPack artifact checks use namespaced typed inputs (`COPPERFIN_ARTIFACT_DIR`, `COPPERFIN_VERSION_FILE`, and `COPPERFIN_EXPECTED_ARTIFACT_SUFFIXES`) and normalize relative roots from the repository source. Keep the generated `CopperfinPackageVersion.txt` path and artifact suffix contracts stable; the focused fixture covers relative success plus missing/invalid version rejection, and hosted Windows evidence remains required.
- Active macOS transaction slice `#4225`: deferred POSIX package resume now reads and compares the marker through the pinned parent descriptor, avoiding a nested `/dev/fd` reopen that fails on macOS. Preserve the existing macOS descriptor identity/no-follow checks, logical returned paths, rollback behavior, and package/debug contracts; hosted macOS validation is still required before closing #4221/#4225.
- Diagnostic follow-up for `#4221`: runtime-pipeline materialization assertions must include the returned localized error, and build-host smoke assertions must retain captured stdout/stderr when expected package output is absent. This is test evidence only; do not change package or machine-readable runtime contracts to make diagnostics easier.
- `#4221` descriptor-source repair: POSIX `copy_file_if_exists` and compiler-contract digest preflights must recognize fd-backed `/proc/self/fd/<descriptor>/...` and `/dev/fd/<descriptor>/...` sources before calling high-level `std::filesystem::exists`. The descriptor-aware readers/copy paths remain authoritative for pinned package files; ordinary source paths retain the normal filesystem path behavior.
- Audit Containment workflow slice `#4237`: the workflow's push and pull-request filters explicitly cover every split source used by `test_runtime_host_debug_output_formatting`, `test_runtime_host_audit_stream`, and the standalone audit-containment target, including shared support, contract, localization, and ordered-main files. Keep the CMake path-filter contract synchronized when these test targets are split again; stale monolithic filenames must not return.
- Native VCX activation slice `#4239` under `#3217`: local `NEWOBJECT("ClassName", "library.vcx", ...)` now opens the shared VCX/VCT model and generates a single-root PRG class bridge consumed by the existing stack-frugal native frame machine. Constructor arguments reach `Init()`, root visual property assignments and methods are preserved, and the actual resolved VCX path is exposed as `ClassLibrary`; missing classes/open failures use catalog-routed diagnostics and native `prg.object.newobject` telemetry. Keep inheritance graphs, child-object materialization, and broad design-time VCX behavior in separate slices; do not route this local path through COM/OLE fallback.
- Runtime-host breakpoint validation slice `#4240` under `#110`: startup `--breakpoint` values now fail closed through the existing localized `RuntimeHost.Debug.Error.InvalidBreakpointCommand` diagnostic and debug error exit code when parsing fails, before the startup PRG executes. Preserve valid line-only/file-line registration, invariant status and breakpoint fields, Windows/POSIX path handling, and the separate interactive `break:add` contract; do not silently discard malformed startup values.
- Runtime manifest security slice `#4241` under `#110`: `security_enabled` is now a strict manifest boolean accepting only `true` or `false`; missing or malformed values fail through the existing localized `RuntimeHost.Error.TrueFalseValueRequired` diagnostic before startup, rather than disabling verification and physical containment. Preserve valid secure/insecure behavior, manifest field names, status/exit contracts, package/debug fields, and the stack-frugal runtime boundary.
- Windows managed wrapper slice `#4242` under `#110`: `.cmd`/`.bat` host launches now pass the complete quoted wrapper command through the nonrecursive `COPPERFIN_SCRIPT_WRAPPER_COMMAND` child environment variable and invoke that variable through `cmd.exe /d /c`, preserving literal percent characters in wrapper paths and forwarded arguments. Preserve direct `.exe`/extensionless and POSIX launches, quoting, empty arguments, localized process diagnostics, and do not replace the indirection with `%%` percent escaping, which is not supported for command-line parsing.
- Managed process-argument quoting slice `#4243` under `#110`: Studio host, project workflow, and runtime debug builders share `CopperfinStudioHostBridge.QuoteProcessArgument`, which applies Windows CRT-compatible backslash/quote quoting for embedded quotes and trailing backslashes. Preserve the separate FoxPro string serializer, wrapper environment indirection, direct/POSIX launches, empty arguments, command order, and machine contracts.
- Automatic runtime-host discovery slice `#4244` under `#110`: build-host sibling and fallback paths must cross `copperfin::platform::path_to_utf8_string` instead of using locale-dependent `std::filesystem::path::string()`. The Windows UTF-8 launcher regression copies both host executables into a non-ASCII directory and launches without `--runtime-host`; preserve explicit overrides, POSIX behavior, and package/debug machine contracts.
- Visual-asset UTF-8 transaction slice `#4245` under `#24`: `src/vfp/visual_asset_editor_shared_helpers.cpp` converts UTF-8 API paths before I/O, builds `.cptmp`, `.cpbak`, and `.cpcommit` with filesystem operations, and returns sidecar/diagnostic paths through `path_to_utf8_string`; `src/vfp/sidecar_path.cpp` uses the same boundary for names and extensions. Preserve case-folded sidecar ambiguity behavior, transaction cleanup/recovery, machine contracts, and the Unicode FRX regression.
- Studio subsystem localization slice `#4246` under `#2348`: `product_subsystems_for_catalog()` keeps invariant `vfp9_equivalent` identifiers stable and translates the additive `vfp9_equivalent_display` through `Studio.ProductSubsystem.<Subsystem>.Vfp9Equivalent`.
