#4621 focused Windows evidence is now green at `81463ac4`: the Release DesignerSmoke rebuild had zero warnings/errors and `SmokeStandaloneStudioToolWindowFloating` passed all 13 assertions, including both floating-state and bounds persistence/restoration. No modal dialog, crash, or WinForms disposal exception appeared; artifact `artifacts/designer-smoke-81463ac4-isolated` was reported by the Windows client. The full Windows Deep Validation run `30149495381` remains in progress and is still required for the broader hosted release gate.
#4621 final floating-state fix is implemented locally: `StudioMainForm` now tracks command and terminal floating state explicitly, restores that logical state before creating forms, and distinguishes user close from WinForms `FormOwnerClosing`. User closure still re-docks and clears the persisted flag; owner shutdown preserves the logical floating flags even if Windows closes owned forms before the main-form save path. The standalone Studio and DesignerSmoke projects build with zero warnings/errors, and portable `SmokeStandaloneStudioToolWindowFloating` passes all 13 assertions. Fresh exact-head Windows validation is required before #4621 can close.
#4621 follow-up is implemented in the Windows deep-smoke harness: the secure RuntimePackage stage now sets `COPPERFIN_SECURITY_ROLE=build-engineer`, and XAsset/Report/Menu stages copy each VFP startup asset plus its `.sct`/`.vct`/`.frt`/`.mnt` sidecar into the declared package root before launching. This addresses harness-level security-role and missing-staged-asset failures reported by the Windows client; the broader exact-head Windows/Visual Studio gate remains open.
#4634 under #4621 is implemented and closed: Windows process-runner timeout fixtures use a 5-second startup budget instead of 1.5 seconds, while retaining bounded cleanup and PID/descendant assertions; the POSIX fixture remains at 300 ms. Exact Windows Deep Validation passes the managed and net472 process-runner stages.
#4635 under #4621 is implemented and closed: `test_studio_host_json` has a CTest `TIMEOUT 600` property because the Windows VFP9 host completed it in 292.74 seconds when isolated, while the ordinary CTest default remains 180 seconds for other tests. Exact Windows Deep Validation passes full native CTest 307/307.
#4636 under #4621 is implemented and closed: affected real FRX/LBX page-setting round-trip tests no longer hard-code five/six/seven root-setting counts, because installed VFP9 samples can expose additional recovered metadata. They still check setting-count field presence plus each edited property's value/provenance and set/clear or undo behavior. The mounted VFP9 native cluster passes 40/40 against real `invoice.frx` and `cust.lbx`.
#4636 follow-up is implemented and closed: the shared real-sample test support parses numeric `settingCount` values. Materializing property suites assert that update adds exactly one setting before clear restores the count; direct margins assert two additions and removal; page-setting updates and command undo compare with the initial sample baseline because those settings already exist in recovered VFP9 metadata. The full mounted-media cluster passes 40/40; exact Windows hosted execution remains an RC evidence item under #4621.
#4621 diagnostic follow-up is implemented locally: `DesignerSmokeTestRunner.Run` catches managed exceptions from individual smoke tests, reports the exception type/message/stack trace, increments the failure count, and still emits the bounded `END` record. This prevents a Windows .NET Framework modal from hiding the underlying failure. The exact `SmokeStandaloneStudioCommandWindowDocking` selector passes under Linux/Mono offscreen; the hosted Windows run must still confirm whether a Windows-specific layout exception remains.
#4621 DesignerSmoke follow-up is implemented locally: `StudioTerminalWindowControl` defers disposal requested during its synchronous `CreateHandle` path, preventing floating/docked tool-window teardown from re-entering WinForms handle creation. Report and label integer property serialization now treats empty input as a clearable value instead of passing an empty string to `Convert.ToInt32`. The managed project builds with zero warnings, and portable exact selectors for CommandWindowDocking, ToolWindowFloating, missing-COLS host update, and the resolved real-asset coverage cluster pass; exact hosted Windows confirmation remains required.
#4632/#4633 closure evidence: exact Windows Native `30150872577` and Windows Deep `30150879972` passed full native CTest 307/307 at product head `bbfccfef`, including the parser/control-flow/runtime targets. The issues are closed; live Visual Studio/UI validation remains tracked separately under #4621.
